## Required debt checks

### msg 230 — pre-sleep ideas

The ideas are recoverable. Msg 563 repeats the three explicitly: **"give characters to the whole team of 10 people!"**, **"Give proper access to the files on Google Drive for debugging"**, and **"Install local Wispr model and process the voice messages from TG! Requires testing and gotta see how much RAM it requires."** Earlier source messages are 147/150/153/156/159/162 (role/persona design, adaptive team and mutual/operator models), 189–198 (Operator Profile and How to Work With Vova artifacts), 210/212 (human qualities for QA/Security lenses and role-diverse GPT consilium), and 237/239 (Whisper voice test). Dispositions: persona/operator-profile material is captured by HR-189/HR-210/HR-212 and tracked operator documents; Drive debugging access is a workboard-referenced debt; Whisper/voice is triaged at msg 239 and has implementation evidence, but the specific audio payload is not recoverable (next section).

### msg 239 — voice transcript

**Unrecoverable from retained evidence.** The inbox stores only `(voice message)`. A bounded host search for audio files around 2026-07-31T07:08Z found none, and the system journal for 06:55–07:25Z contains daemon connection events but no msg 239, audio path, Whisper invocation, or transcript. Mailbox evidence proves Whisper was installed/tested later, not that this payload or transcript was retained. Proposed row: **Recover or explicitly close missing Telegram voice payload 239**; verbatim capture: **"(voice message)"**.

### msg 788 — Gmail-sync doubt

The prior claim was stronger than the retained evidence supports. The old two-sided log claimed: **"рахунок із Gmail реально доїхав у Drive у 2026 / 2026-06"**. The overnight report's W-21 row instead proves production contained incompatible/orphan Gmail tables and a phantom `002_gmail_import_storage.sql` migration; it does not prove a current app Gmail extraction, destination Shared Drive, or retained extracted dataset. The durable evidence path is `reports/overnight-2026-08-01.md`, W-21. Therefore the defensible disposition is: historical claim pointed to a then-observed Drive path, while current reconciliation evidence went to PostgreSQL orphan tables renamed `*_orphan_20260801`; no durable evidence ties msg 788's claimed extraction to an app-connected ShareDrive. Proposed row: **Re-prove Gmail extraction destination end-to-end on the connected application stand**; verbatim capture: **"Ти казав, що ти запускав уже якісь там синхронізації, витягування з Gmail. А куди ти його витягав? У нас же навіть ShareDrive не підключено до аплікухи."**

## Reproduction

Inputs were read-only. Counts can be reproduced with:

```sh
wc -l /root/bpa-dev-infrastructure/instance/decisions/inbox.jsonl
grep -c '^=== message_id=' /root/orch-mailbox/vova-telegram-archive/vova-telegram-by-message-id.txt
```
